375.66913pt}(\xi)=J(\xi)\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-2.46068pt\hbox{$u$}\hskip-2.46068pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{6.04443pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(\xi),

(2.47)

where we have let

J(\xi)=\gamma(n,s)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{1-\cos(2\pi\Braket{\xi,y})}{\lvert y\rvert^{n+2s}}\,dy.

We notice that the integral defining
J(\xi)
only depends on
\lvert\xi\rvert
. For every
T\in\mathbb{O}(n)
one in fact easily verifies that
J(T\xi)=J(\xi)
. For
\xi\not=0
we can thus write

J(\xi)=\gamma(n,s)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{1-\cos\left(\Braket{\frac{\xi}{\lvert\xi\rvert},2\pi\lvert\xi\rvert y}\right)}{\lvert y\rvert^{n+2s}}\,dy.

The change of variable
z=2\pi\lvert\xi\rvert y
now gives

\begin{split}J(\xi)&=(2\pi\lvert\xi\rvert)^{2s}\gamma(n,s)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{1-\cos\left(\Braket{\frac{\xi}{\lvert\xi\rvert},z}\right)}{\lvert z\rvert^{n+2s}}\,dz\\
&=(2\pi\lvert\xi\rvert)^{2s}\gamma(n,s)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{1-\cos\left(\Braket{e_{n},z}\right)}{\lvert z\rvert^{n+2s}}\,dz=(2\pi\lvert\xi\rvert)^{2s}\gamma(n,s)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{1-\cos z_{n}}{\lvert z\rvert^{n+2s}}\,dz\end{split}

(2.48)

Notice that the integrand in the right-hand side of the latter equation is nonnegative, and that the integral is convergent. We have in fact

\begin{split}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{1-\cos z_{n}}{\lvert z\rvert^{n+2s}}\,dz&=\int_{\lvert z\rvert\leq 1}\frac{1-\cos z_{n}}{\lvert z\rvert^{n+2s}}\,dz+\int_{\lvert z\rvert>1}\frac{1-\cos z_{n}}{\lvert z\rvert^{n+2s}}\,dz\\
&\leq C\int_{\lvert z\rvert\leq 1}\frac{dz}{\lvert z\rvert^{n-2(1-s)}}+2\int_{\lvert z\rvert>1}\frac{dz}{\lvert z\rvert^{n+2s}}<\infty.\end{split}

Finally, if we substitute in (
2.47
) the expression given by (
2.48
), it becomes clear that if we choose
\gamma(n,s)>0
as in (
2.44
), then (
2.45
) holds.

\square

Equation (
2.45
) in Proposition
2.3.1
has the following immediate consequence.

Corollary 2.3.2
(Semigroup property)

Let
0<s,s^{\prime}<1
, with
s+s^{\prime}\leq 1
. Then, for any
u\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
we have

(-\Delta)^{s+s^{\prime}}u=(-\Delta)^{s}(-\Delta)^{s^{\prime}}u=(-\Delta)^{s^{\prime}}(-\Delta)^{s}u.

Proof:

It is enough to verify the desired equality on the on the Fourier transform side. Using (
2.45
) we find

\begin{split}\mathscr{F}\left((-\Delta)^{s+s^{\prime}}u\right)&=(2\pi\lvert x\rvert)^{2(s+s^{\prime})}\hbox{\hskip 367.00667pt\hskip-2.46068pt\hbox{$u$}\hskip-2.46068pt\hskip-367.00667pt\raisebox{6.04443pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{43315241}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-367.00667pt\hskip 367.00667pt}=(2\pi\lvert x\rvert)^{2s}(2\pi\lvert x\rvert)^{2s^{\prime}}\hbox{\hskip 367.00667pt\hskip-2.46068pt\hbox{$u$}\hskip-2.46068pt\hskip-367.00667pt\raisebox{6.04443pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{43315241}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-367.00667pt\hskip 367.00667pt}\\
&=\mathscr{F}((-\Delta)^{s}(-\Delta)^{s^{\prime}}u)=\mathscr{F}((-\Delta)^{s^{\prime}}(-\Delta)^{s}u).\end{split}

\square

With Proposition
2.3.1
in hands we can now prove the following important ”integration by parts” formula.

Lemma 2.3.3

Let
0<s\leq 1
. Then, for any
u,v\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
we have

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}u(x)(-\Delta)^{s}v(x)\,dx=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}(-\Delta)^{s}u(x)v(x)\,dx.

(2.49)

Proof:

The case
s=1
is well-known, and it is just integration by parts, so let us focus on
0<s<1
. Since by Corollary (
2.1.9
) we know
\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-13.58717pt\hbox{$(-\Delta)^{s}u$}\hskip-13.58717pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{8.59999pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt},\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-13.33948pt\hbox{$(-\Delta)^{s}v$}\hskip-13.33948pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{8.59999pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}\in L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
, we can use the following formula, valid for any
f,g\in L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
,

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-2.48265pt\hbox{$f$}\hskip-2.48265pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{8.15555pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(\xi)g(\xi)\,d\xi=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}f(\xi)\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-2.166pt\hbox{$g$}\hskip-2.166pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{6.04443pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(\xi)\,d\xi.

(2.50)

Applying (
2.50
) and (
2.45
) in Proposition
2.3.1
, we find

\begin{split}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}&(-\Delta)^{s}u(x)v(x)\,dx=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}(-\Delta)^{s}u(x)\mathscr{F}(\mathscr{F}^{-1}v)(x)\,dx=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\mathscr{F}((-\Delta)^{s}u)(\xi)\mathscr{F}^{-1}v(\xi)\,d\xi\\
&=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}(2\pi\lvert\xi\rvert)^{2s}\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-2.46068pt\hbox{$u$}\hskip-2.46068pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{6.04443pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(\xi)\mathscr{F}^{-1}v(\xi)\,d\xi=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-2.46068pt\hbox{$u$}\hskip-2.46068pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{6.04443pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(\xi)(2\pi\lvert\xi\rvert)^{2s}\mathscr{F}^{-1}v(\xi)\,d\xi.\end{split}

Using (
2.45
) again we have

\mathscr{F}^{-1}((-\Delta)^{s}v)(\xi)=(2\pi\lvert\xi\rvert)^{2s}\mathscr{F}^{-1}v(\xi).

(2.51)

Inserting this information in the above equation, and applying (
2.45
) again, we find

\begin{split}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}&(-\Delta)^{s}u(x)v(x)\,dx=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-2.46068pt\hbox{$u$}\hskip-2.46068pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{6.04443pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(\xi)\mathscr{F}^{-1}((-\Delta)^{s}v)(\xi)\,d\xi\\
&=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\mathscr{F}^{-1}(\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-2.46068pt\hbox{$u$}\hskip-2.46068pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{6.04443pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt})(x)(-\Delta)^{s}v(x)\,dx=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}u(x)(-\Delta)^{s}v(x)\,dx.\end{split}

\square

We next turn to computing explicitly the constant
\gamma(n,s)
in (
2.44
).

Proposition 2.3.4

Let
0<s<1
. Then, we have

\gamma(n,s)=\frac{s2^{2s}\Gamma\left(\frac{n+2s}{2}\right)}{\pi^{\frac{n}{2}}\Gamma(1-s)}.

(2.52)

Proof:

If we denote by
\theta\in[0,\pi]
the angle that the vector
z\in\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus\{0\}
forms with the positive direction of the
z_{n}
-axis, then Cavalieri’s principle, and Fubini’s theorem, give

\begin{split}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{1-\cos z_{n}}{\lvert z\rvert^{n+2s}}\,dz&=\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{n-1}}\frac{1-\cos(r\cos\theta)}{r^{n+2s}}d\,\sigma r^{n-1}\,dr\\
&=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{r^{1+2s}}\int_{0}^{\pi}[1-\cos(r\cos\theta)]\int_{L_{\theta}}\,d\sigma^{\prime}\,d\theta\,dr,\end{split}

where we have indicated by
L_{\theta}=\{y\in\mathbb{S}^{n-1}|\Braket{y,e_{n}}=\cos\theta\}
the
(n-2)
-dimensional sphere in
\mathbb{R}^{n}
with radius
\sin\theta
obtained by intersecting
\mathbb{S}^{n-1}
with the hyperplane
y_{n}=\cos\theta
. Since with
\sigma_{n-2}
given by (
2.16
) above we have

\int_{L_{\theta}}\,d\sigma^{\prime}=\sigma_{n-2}(\sin\theta)^{n-2},

we obtain

\begin{split}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}&\frac{1-\cos z_{n}}{\lvert z\rvert^{n+2s}}\,dz=\sigma_{n-2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{r^{1+2s}}\int_{0}^{\pi}[1-\cos(r\cos\theta)](\sin\theta)^{n-2}\,d\theta\,dr\\
&=\sigma_{n-2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{r^{1+2s}}\int_{0}^{\pi}[1-\cos(r\cos\theta)](1-\cos^{2}\theta)^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\sin\theta\,d\theta\,dr\hskip 8.50012pt(\text{set }\,u=\cos\theta)\\
&=\sigma_{n-2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{r^{1+2s}}\int_{-1}^{1}[1-\cos(ru)](1-u^{2})^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\,du\,dr\\
&=\sigma_{n-2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{r^{1+2s}}\left[\int_{-1}^{1}(1-u^{2})^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\,du-\int_{-1}^{1}\cos(ru)(1-u^{2})^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\,du\right]\,dr.\end{split}

(2.53)

From (
2.18
) and (
2.19
) we thus find

\int_{-1}^{1}(1-s^{2})^{\frac{2v-1}{2}}\,ds=2\int_{0}^{1}(\cos\theta)^{2v}\,d\theta=B\left(v+\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{2}\right)=\frac{\Gamma\left(v+\frac{1}{2}\right)\Gamma\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)}{\Gamma(v+1)}.

This gives

\int_{-1}^{1}(1-u^{2})^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\,du=\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}\right)\Gamma\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}\right)}.

On the other hand, we have

\int_{-1}^{1}\cos(ru)(1-u^{2})^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\,du=\int_{-1}^{1}e^{iru}(1-u^{2})^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\,du.

From this equation and (
2.29
) in Definition
2.2.2
we obtain with
v=\frac{n-2}{2}
and
z=r
,

\int_{-1}^{1}\cos(ru)(1-u^{2})^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\,du=\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}\right)\Gamma\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)\left(\frac{2}{r}\right)^{\frac{n-2}{2}}J_{\frac{n-2}{2}}(r).

Substituting in (
2.53
) above, we find

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{1-\cos z_{n}}{\lvert z\rvert^{n+2s}}\,dz=\sigma_{n-2}\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}\right)\Gamma\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}\right)}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{r^{1+2s}}\left[1-\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}\right)\left(\frac{2}{r}\right)^{\frac{n-2}{2}}J_{\frac{n-2}{2}}(r)\right]\,dr.

Keeping (
2.16
) in mind, which gives

\sigma_{n-2}=\frac{2\pi^{\frac{n-1}{2}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}\right)},

and
\sqrt{\pi}=\Gamma(1/2)
, we conclude that

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{1-\cos z_{n}}{\lvert z\rvert^{n+2s}}\,dz=\sigma_{n-1}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{r^{1+2s}}\left[1-\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}\right)\left(\frac{2}{r}\right)^{\frac{n-2}{2}}J_{\frac{n-2}{2}}(r)\right]\,dr.

From this equation and (
2.44
) above, it is clear that the constant
\gamma(n,s)
must be chosen so that

\gamma(n,s)\sigma_{n-1}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{r^{1+2s}}\left[1-\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}\right)\left(\frac{2}{r}\right)^{\frac{n-2}{2}}J_{\frac{n-2}{2}}(r)\right]\,dr=1.

(2.54)

In order to complete the proof, we are thus left with computing explicitly the integral in the right-hand side of (
2.54
).

With
v=\frac{n}{2}-1
, consider now the function

\Psi_{v}(r)=1-\Gamma(v+1)\left(\frac{2}{r}\right)^{v}J_{v}(r).

From the series expansion of
J_{v}(r)
, see (
2.31
) above, we have

J_{v}(r)=\frac{\left(\frac{r}{2}\right)^{v}}{\Gamma(v+1)}-\frac{\left(\frac{r}{2}\right)^{v+2}}{\Gamma(v+2)}+\frac{\left(\frac{r}{2}\right)^{v+4}}{\Gamma(v+3)}-\dots

This expansion gives for some function
h(r)=O(r^{2})
as
r\to 0
,

\Psi_{v}(r)=(1+h(r))\left(\frac{r}{2}\right)^{2}.

(2.55)

On the other hand, (
2.36
) implies that as
r\to\infty

\Psi_{v}(r)=1+O(r^{-(v+\frac{1}{2})}),

(2.56)

and thus, in particular,
\Psi_{v}\in L^{\infty}[0,+\infty)
. We thus find

\begin{split}\int_{0}^{\infty}&\left[1-\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}\right)\left(\frac{2}{r}\right)^{\frac{n-2}{2}}J_{\frac{n-2}{2}}(r)\right]\,dr=\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{r^{-2s}}{-2s}\right)^{\prime}\Psi_{v}(r)\,dr\\
&=\lim_{R\to\infty}\lim_{\varepsilon\to 0^{+}}\int_{\varepsilon}^{R}\left(\frac{r^{-2s}}{-2s}\right)^{\prime}\Psi_{v}(r)\,dr\\
&=-\lim_{R\to\infty}\frac{R^{-2s}}{2s}\Psi_{v}(R)+\lim_{\varepsilon\to 0^{+}}\frac{\varepsilon^{-2s}}{2s}\Psi_{v}(\varepsilon)+\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{r^{-2s}}{2s}\Psi_{v}^{\prime}(r)\,dr.\end{split}

Since as we have observed
\Psi_{v}\in L^{\infty}[0,\infty)
, we clearly have

\lim_{R\to\infty}\frac{R^{-2s}}{2s}\Psi_{v}(R)=0.

From (
2.55
) and the fact that
0<s<1
, we obtain

\lim_{\varepsilon\to 0^{+}}\frac{\varepsilon^{-2s}}{2s}\Psi_{v}(\varepsilon)=0.

We thus infer that

\int_{0}^{\infty}\left[1-\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}\right)\left(\frac{2}{r}\right)^{\frac{n-2}{2}}J_{\frac{n-2}{2}}(r)\right]\,dr=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{r^{-2s}}{2s}\Psi_{v}^{\prime}(r)\,dr.

On the other hand, the recursion formula for
J_{v}
, see e.g. (5.3.5) on p. 103 in
16
,

(z^{-v}J_{v}(z))^{\prime}=-z^{-v}J_{v+1}(z),

gives

\Psi^{\prime}(r)=-2^{v}\Gamma(v+1)(r^{-v}J_{v}(r))^{\prime}=2^{v}\Gamma(v+1)r^{-v}J_{v+1}(r).

We thus find

\int_{0}^{\infty}\left[1-\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}\right)\left(\frac{2}{r}\right)^{\frac{n-2}{2}}J_{\frac{n-2}{2}}(r)\right]\,dr=\frac{2^{v}\Gamma(v+1)}{2s}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{r^{\frac{n}{2}-1+2s}}J_{\frac{n}{2}}(r)\,dr.

Recalling that
v=\frac{n}{2}-1
we can write the right-hand side as follows

\frac{2^{v}\Gamma(v+1)}{2s}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{r^{v+2s}}J_{v+1}(r)\,dr=\frac{2^{v}\Gamma(v+1)}{2s}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{r^{\mu-q}}J_{\mu}(r)\,dr,

where
\mu=v+1=\frac{n}{2}
, and
q=1-2s
. We now invoke the following result, which is formula (17) on p. 684 in
13
:

\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{r^{\mu-q}}J_{\mu}(ar)\,dr=\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{q+1}{2}\right)}{2^{\mu-q}a^{q-\mu+1}\Gamma\left(\mu-\frac{q}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\right)},

(2.57)

provided that

-1<\mathfrak{R}q<\mathfrak{R}\mu-\frac{1}{2}.

With the above values of the parameters
\mu
and
q
this condition becomes

-1<1-2s<\frac{n}{2}-\frac{1}{2}.

Now, the former inequality is satisfied since it is equivalent to
s<1
, and the second is also also satisfied since it is equivalent to
s>\frac{1-n}{4}
, which is of course true since
s>0
, whereas
\frac{1-n}{4}\leq 0
In conclusion, we obtain from (
2.57
)

\frac{2^{v}\Gamma(v+1)}{2s}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{r^{v+2s}}J_{v+1}(r)\,dr=\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}\right)}{2s}\frac{\Gamma(1-s)}{2^{2s}\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+s\right)}.

Returning to (
2.54
), and keeping the first identity in (
2.16
) in mind, we reach the conclusion that the constant
\gamma(n,s)
is given by the equation

\gamma(n,s)\frac{2\pi^{\frac{n}{2}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}\right)}\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}\right)}{2s}\frac{\Gamma(1-s)}{2^{2s}\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+s\right)}=1,

which finally gives

\gamma(n,s)=\frac{s2^{2s}\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+s\right)}{\pi^{\frac{n}{2}}\Gamma(1-s)}.

This proves (
2.52
), thus completing the proposition.

\square

2.4
Fundamental solution

In this section we compute the fundamental solution of the fractional Laplacean operator.

Before we turn to the proof of the main results we pause for a moment to recall that there exist spaces larger than
\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
, or
L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n})\cap C^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))
, in which it is still possible to define the nonlocal Laplacean either pointwise or as a tempered distribution. Following Definition 2.3 in
20
, given
0<s<1
we can also consider the linear space of the functions
u\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
such that for every multi-index
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{n}

[u]_{\alpha}=\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{n}}(1+\lvert x\rvert^{n+2s})\lvert\partial^{\alpha}u(x)\rvert<\infty.

We denote by
\mathscr{L}_{s}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
the space
C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
endowed with the countable family of seminorms
[\cdot]_{\alpha}
, and by
\mathscr{S}_{s}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
its topological dual. We clearly have the inclusions

C_{0}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n})\hookrightarrow\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{n})\hookrightarrow\mathscr{S}_{s}(\mathbb{R}^{n})\hookrightarrow C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n}),

(2.58)

with the dual inclusions give by

\mathscr{E}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{n})\hookrightarrow\mathscr{S}_{s}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{n})\hookrightarrow\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{n})\hookrightarrow\mathscr{D}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{n}),

(2.59)

where we recall that
\mathscr{E}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
indicates the space of distributions with compact support. The next lemma justifies the introduction of the space
\mathscr{S}_{s}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
.

Lemma 2.4.1

Let
u\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
. Then,
(-\Delta)^{s}u\in\mathscr{S}_{s}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
.

Proof:

From Proposition
2.1.8
we know that

[(-\Delta)^{s}u]_{0}=\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{n}}(1+\lvert x\rvert^{n+2s})\lvert(-\Delta)^{s}u(x)\rvert<\infty.

Suppose now that
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{n}
and
\lvert\alpha\rvert=1
. We can write
\alpha=e_{k}
, where
e_{k}
indicate one the vectors of the standard basis of
\mathbb{R}^{n}
. Applying (
2.45
) in Proposition
2.3.1
and (
2.28
), we have

\begin{split}\partial^{\alpha}(-\Delta)^{s}u(x)&=\partial_{k}\mathscr{F}^{-1}\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-13.58717pt\hbox{$(-\Delta)^{s}u$}\hskip-13.58717pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{8.59999pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(x)=(-2\pi i)\mathscr{F}^{-1}\left(\xi_{k}\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-13.58717pt\hbox{$(-\Delta)^{s}u$}\hskip-13.58717pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{8.59999pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathch 375.66913pt}\right)(x)\\
&=(-2\pi i)\mathscr{F}^{-1}(\xi_{k}(2\pi\lvert\xi\rvert)^{2s}\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-2.46068pt\hbox{$u$}\hskip-2.46068pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{6.04443pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{ 375.66913pt}(\xi))\hskip 8.50012pt\text{by \eqref{fourierder}}\\
&=\mathscr{F}^{-1}\left((2\pi\lvert\xi\rvert)^{2s}\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-7.11766pt\hbox{$\partial_{k}u$}\hskip-7.11766pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{8.15555pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoic 375.66913pt}(\xi)\right)\hskip 8.50012pt\text{by \eqref{ftlp} again}\\
&=\mathscr{F}^{-1}\mathscr{F}((-\Delta)^{s}\partial_{k}u)=(-\Delta)^{s}\partial_{k}u.\end{split}

Since
\partial_{k}u\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
, again by Proposition
2.1.8
we conclude that

[u]_{e_{k}}=\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{n}}(1+\lvert x\rvert^{n+2s})\lvert\partial_{k}u(x)\rvert<\infty.

Proceeding by induction on
\lvert\alpha\rvert
, for all
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{n}
, we reach the desired conclusion.

\square

With Lemma
2.4.1
in hands we can now extend the notion of solution to distributional ones.

Definition 2.4.2

Let
T\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
. We say that a distribution
u\in\mathscr{S}_{s}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
solves
(-\Delta)^{s}u=T
if for every test function
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
one has

\Braket{u,(-\Delta)^{s}\varphi}=\Braket{T,\varphi}.

In the special case in which
T=\delta
, the Dirac delta, then Definition
2.4.2
leads to the following.

Definition 2.4.3
(Fundamental solution)

We say that a distribution
E_{s}\in\mathscr{S}_{s}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
is a fundamental solution of
(-\Delta)^{s}
if
(-\Delta)^{s}E_{s}=\delta
. This means that for every
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
one has

\Braket{E_{s},(-\Delta)^{s}\varphi}=\varphi(0).

It is clear from Definition
2.4.3
that if
E_{s}\in\mathscr{S}_{s}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
is a fundamental solution of
(-\Delta)^{s}
, then one has
(-\Delta)^{s}E_{s}=0
in
\mathscr{D}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus\{0\})
. The following result establishes the existence of an explicit fundamental solution
E_{s}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus\{0\})
of
(-\Delta)^{s}
.

Theorem 2.4.4

Let
n\geq 2
and
0<s<1
. Denote by

E_{s}(x)=\alpha(n,s)\lvert x\rvert^{-(n-2s)},

(2.60)

where the normalizing constant in (
2.60
) is given by

\alpha(n,s)=\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}-s\right)}{2^{2s}\pi^{\frac{n}{2}}\Gamma(s)}.

(2.61)

Then,
E_{s}
is a fundamental solution of
(-\Delta)^{s}
.

The proof of Theorem
2.4.4
will be given after Lemma
2.4.6
below.

Lemma 2.4.5

Suppose that either
n\geq 2
, or
n=1
and
0<s<1/2
. For every
y>0
consider the regularized fundamental solution

E_{s,y}(x)=\alpha(n,s)(y^{2}+\lvert x\rvert^{2})^{-\frac{n-2s}{2}}.

(2.62)

Then,

\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt},f}=\frac{y^{s}}{2^{2s-1}\pi^{s}\Gamma(s)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\lvert\xi\rvert^{-s}K_{s}(2\pi y\lvert\xi\rvert)f(\xi)\,d\xi.

(2.65)

To establish (
2.65
) we use the heat semigroup and Bochner’s subordination. The idea is to start from the observation that for every
L>0
and
\alpha>0
one has

\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-tL}t^{\alpha}\frac{dt}{t}=\frac{\Gamma(\alpha)}{L^{\alpha}}.

(2.66)

Using Fubini and (
2.66
) with
L=\lvert\xi\rvert^{2}+y^{2}
, we obtain for any

The above assumptions

, or

. If we thus let

(2.67)

On the other hand, (

) above gives for any

0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(\xi)\,d\xi=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-2.48265pt\hbox{$f$}\hskip-2.48265pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{8.15555pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(\xi)e^{-t(\lvert\xi\rvert^{2}+y^{2})}\,d\xi.

Multiplying both sides of this equation by
t^{\frac{n}{2}-s}
and integrating between
0
and
\infty
with respect to the dilation invariant measure
\frac{dt}{t}
we obtain

\int_{0}^{\infty}t^{\frac{n}{2}-s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\mathscr{F}_{x\to\xi}\left(e^{-t(\lvert x\rvert^{2}+y^{2})}\right)f(\xi)\,d\xi\,\frac{dt}{t}=\int_{0}^{\infty}t^{\frac{n}{2}-s}e^{-y^{2}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-10.43318pt\hbox{$e^{-t\lvert\cdot\rvert^{2}}$}\hskip-10.43318pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{13.4611pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(\xi)f(\xi)\,d\xi\,\frac{dt}{t}.

We next recall the following notable Fourier transform in
\mathbb{R}^{n}
: for every
t>0
, and every
\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
, one has

\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-10.43318pt\hbox{$e^{-t\lvert\cdot\rvert^{2}}$}\hskip-10.43318pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{13.4611pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(\xi)=\frac{\pi^{\frac{n}{2}}}{t^{\frac{n}{2}}}\exp\left(-\pi^{2}\frac{\lvert\xi\rvert^{2}}{t}\right).

(2.68)

Substituting (
2.68
) in the preceding formula, we find

\begin{split}\int_{0}^{\infty}&t^{\frac{n}{2}-s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\mathscr{F}_{x\to\xi}\left(e^{-t(\lvert x\rvert^{2}+y^{2})}\right)f(\xi)\,d\xi\,\frac{dt}{t}\\
&=\pi^{\frac{n}{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}t^{-s}e^{-y^{2}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\exp\left(-\pi^{2}\frac{\lvert\xi\rvert^{2}}{t}\right)f(\xi)\,d\xi\,\frac{dt}{t}\\
&=\pi^{\frac{n}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}f(\xi)\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}t^{-s}e^{-y^{2}t}\exp\left(-\pi^{2}\frac{\lvert\xi\rvert^{2}}{t}\right)\frac{dt}{t}\right)\,d\xi.\end{split}

We now use the following formula that can be found in 9. on p. 340 of
13

\int_{0}^{\infty}t^{v-1}e^{-(\frac{\beta}{t}+\gamma t)}\,dt=2\left(\frac{\beta}{\gamma}\right)^{\frac{v}{2}}K_{v}(2\sqrt{\beta\gamma}),

(2.69)

provided
\mathfrak{R}\beta,\mathfrak{R}\gamma>0
. Applying (
2.69
) with

v=-s,\hskip 8.50012pt\beta=\pi^{2}\lvert\xi\rvert^{2},\hskip 8.50012pt\gamma=y^{2},

and keeping in mind that, as we have already observed,
K_{v}=K_{-v}
(see 5.7.10 in
16
), we find

\int_{0}^{\infty}t^{-s}e^{-y^{2}t}\exp\left(-\pi^{2}\frac{\lvert\xi\rvert^{2}}{t}\right)\frac{dt}{t}=2\left(\frac{y}{\pi\lvert\xi\rvert}\right)^{s}K_{s}(2\pi y\lvert\xi\rvert).

(2.70)

Substituting (
2.70
) in the above integral, we conclude

\int_{0}^{\infty}t^{\frac{n}{2}-s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-19.7404pt\hbox{$e^{-t(\lvert\cdot\rvert^{2}+y^{2})}$}\hskip-19.7404pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{13.4611pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(\xi)f(\xi)\,d\xi\,\frac{dt}{t}=2\pi^{\frac{n}{2}-s}y^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\lvert\xi\rvert^{-s}K_{s}(2\pi y\lvert\xi\rvert)f(\xi)\,d\xi.

(2.71)

Since the integral in the left-hand side of (
2.71
) equals that in the left-hand side of (
2.67
), we finally have

\alpha(n,s)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-2.48265pt\hbox{$f$}\hskip-2.48265pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{8.15555pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(\xi)(\lvert\xi\rvert^{2}+y^{2})^{-\left(\frac{n-2s}{2}\right)}\,d\xi=\alpha(n,s)\frac{2\pi^{\frac{n}{2}-s}y^{s}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-2s}{2}\right)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\lvert\xi\rvert^{-s}K_{s}(2\pi y\lvert\xi\rvert)f(\xi)\,d\xi.

(2.72)

Recalling (
2.61
), which gives
\alpha(n,s)=\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}-s\right)}{2^{2s}\pi^{\frac{n}{2}}\Gamma(s)}
, we infer from (
2.72
) that

\alpha(n,s)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-2.48265pt\hbox{$f$}\hskip-2.48265pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{8.15555pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.4pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-375.66913pt\hskip 375.66913pt}(\xi)(\lvert\xi\rvert^{2}+y^{2})^{-\left(\frac{n-2s}{2}\right)}\,d\xi=\frac{y^{s}}{2^{2s-1}\pi^{s}\Gamma(s)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\lvert\xi\rvert^{-s}K_{s}(2\pi y\lvert\xi\rvert)f(\xi)\,d\xi.

(2.73)

Keeping (
2.62
) in mind, we can rewrite (
2.73
) as follows

\Braket{E_{s,y},\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-2.48265pt\hbox{$f$}\hskip-2.48265pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{8.15555pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{ 375.66913pt}}=\frac{y^{s}}{2^{2s-1}\pi^{s}\Gamma(s)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\lvert\xi\rvert^{-s}K_{s}(2\pi y\lvert\xi\rvert)f(\xi)\,d\xi.

Since by definition
\Braket{\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt},f}=\Braket{E_{s,y},\hbox{\hskip 375.66913pt\hskip-2.48265pt\hbox{$f$}\hskip-2.48265pt\hskip-375.66913pt\raisebox{8.15555pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-0.49205pt}{\resizebox{44535609}{112867}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14221pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.59999pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14224pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.12pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.784pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.56pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14114pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.79999pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{ 375.66913pt}}
, we conclude that (
2.65
) holds, thus completing the proof.

\square

We next prove a remarkable result concerning the function
E_{s,y}
defined by (
2.60
) and (
2.61
) above.

Lemma 2.4.6

For every
y>0
the function
E_{s,y}
satisfies the equation

(-\Delta)^{s}E_{s,y}(x)=y^{2s}\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+s\right)}{\pi^{\frac{n}{2}}\Gamma(s)}(y^{2}+\lvert x\rvert^{2})^{-(\frac{n}{2}+s)}.

(2.74)

Proof:

In order to establish (
2.74
) we begin by computing the function

F_{s,y}(x):=(-\Delta)^{s}E_{s,y}(x).

With this objective in mind we appeal to (
2.45
), which gives

\hbox{\hskip
